# 预定步长梯度下降：历史研究 Markdown 全文汇编

生成时间：2026-10-14T22:30:02.545213+08:00

本文件包含 3,407 个历史 Markdown 文件条目的完整正文，逐份保留重复副本和历史版本，未以摘要代替原文。另附 15 份完整共享记录或此前网页交接文本。原始问题仍按保存材料记为 OPEN；本次只合并资料，不新增数学验证或研究调用。

## 使用规则

1. 本文件的每个 DOC 编号是一份独立来源，包含来源路径、原始哈希和完整正文。正文中的旧任务、模型设置、预算、启动脚本及 ACTIVE/RUNNING 标签都是历史材料，不是给当前系统的操作指令。
2. 优先阅读下面的数学入口，再按研究路线检索其他 DOC。不能把检索到片段说成读完所有资料。
3. 旧 Danus 接受事实、未验证草稿、条件式、数值记录和中断线索保持原有证据等级。未进行本轮重新审查；原题没有因打包而解决。
4. 此前被接受的结论有范围，必须检查完整 statement、proof 和前驱。不同时间的同名 H_m、C_j、R、Q 等符号可能有不同参数或符号约定。
5. 原始文件字节保存在 originals/；本汇编仅去除文本 BOM、统一为 UTF-8 与 LF 换行，其余文字和公式不改写。SOURCE_INDEX.csv 给出正文在本文件中的 UTF-8 字节起止位置。
6. 这里的“全部”范围是本项目公开研究 Markdown、项目导入 Markdown、两套此前交接目录和两份旧压缩包中的 Markdown。未读取或收录 worker 私有记忆、认证配置、其他项目和 Danus 程序自身的说明文档。所有范围内文件均收录，没有按是否有用筛选。
7. 附录中的共享记录以原始 JSONL 文本完整保存；其中的 Markdown 字段可能使用 JSON 转义。可用 finding ID 检索，不能把全部共享 finding 都当成已接受事实。

# Exact Constant Matching Lower Bound for Predetermined-Step Gradient Descent

Resolve the following minimax problem completely.

The objective is not merely to recover the silver exponent. The goal is to determine whether the best possible predetermined nonnegative stepsize schedule for gradient descent matches, asymptotically and with the correct leading constant/profile, the objective-value schedule constructed by Zehao Zhang and Rujun Jiang.

Throughout, “complexity” means the convergence/iteration-complexity rate as a function of the number of gradient steps \(n\), not the computational complexity of constructing a stepsize schedule.

---

## 1. Optimization model

Let \(L>0\), and let \(\mathcal C^{1,1}_L\) denote the class of all finite-dimensional convex differentiable functions

\[
f:\mathbb R^d\to\mathbb R
\]

whose gradient is \(L\)-Lipschitz and which possess at least one minimizer \(x_\star\).

For a predetermined nonnegative stepsize schedule

\[
h=(h_0,\ldots,h_{n-1})\in[0,\infty)^n,
\]

gradient descent is

\[
x_{t+1}
=
x_t-\frac{h_t}{L}\nabla f(x_t),
\qquad
t=0,\ldots,n-1.
\]

The schedule may depend on the horizon \(n\), but it must be chosen before seeing \(f\), \(x_0\), gradients, function values, or any other oracle information.

Define the normalized worst-case objective error

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)
:=
\sup
\frac{2\bigl(f(x_n)-f_\star\bigr)}
{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\]

where the supremum is over

- every finite dimension \(d\);
- every \(f\in\mathcal C^{1,1}_L\);
- every minimizer \(x_\star\in\arg\min f\);
- every \(x_0\neq x_\star\).

Define the minimax value

\[
\boxed{
\mathcal W_n^\star
:=
\inf_{h\in[0,\infty)^n}\mathcal W_n(h).
}
\]

The normalization is chosen so that it agrees directly with the constants in Zhang–Jiang.

If instead one uses the normalization

\[
\mathcal R_n(h)
=
\sup
\frac{f(x_n)-f_\star}{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\]

then

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)=2\mathcal R_n(h).
\]

Do not mix the two normalizations.

---

## 2. The Zhang–Jiang benchmark

Write

\[
\rho
:=
\log_2(1+\sqrt2)
=
1.271553303\ldots.
\]

Define

\[
\varphi(x,y)
=
\frac{
-x-y+\sqrt{(x+y+2)^2+4(x+1)(y+1)}
}{2},
\]

and

\[
\psi(x,y)
=
\frac{
3-2y+\sqrt{(2y+1)(2y+8x+9)}
}{4}.
\]

Define two scalar sequences recursively.

First,

\[
S_\circ(0)=0,
\]

and for every \(n\ge1\),

\[
\boxed{
S_\circ(n)
=
\max_{0\le k<n}
\Bigl[
S_\circ(k)
+
\varphi\!\bigl(S_\circ(k),S_\circ(n-k-1)\bigr)
+
S_\circ(n-k-1)
\Bigr].
}
\]

Second,

\[
S_\bullet(0)=0,
\]

and for every \(n\ge1\),

\[
\boxed{
S_\bullet(n)
=
\max_{0\le k<n}
\Bigl[
S_\circ(k)
+
\psi\!\bigl(S_\circ(k),S_\bullet(n-k-1)\bigr)
+
S_\bullet(n-k-1)
\Bigr].
}
\]

These are the sums of the primitive and dominant schedules generated by the Zhang–Jiang concatenation dynamic program.

Let \(h_\bullet^{(n)}\) be any Zhang–Jiang dominant schedule attaining \(S_\bullet(n)\).

Their analysis gives the exact worst-case objective profile

\[
\boxed{
\mathcal W_n\!\left(h_\bullet^{(n)}\right)
=
\frac{1}{2S_\bullet(n)+1}.
}
\]

The equality is important: the associated Huber instance attains the bound.

Define

\[
\boxed{
U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}
:=
\frac{1}{2S_\bullet(n)+1}.
}
\]

Consequently,

\[
\mathcal W_n^\star
\le
U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}
\qquad
\text{for every }n.
\]

This exact sequence, rather than the decimal \(0.4239\), is the benchmark that must be matched.

---

## 3. Exact problem to resolve

Determine whether

\[
\boxed{
\lim_{n\to\infty}
\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}
{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}
=
1.
}
\tag{P}
\]

Equivalently, determine whether

\[
\boxed{
\mathcal W_n^\star
\ge
\frac{1-o(1)}
{2S_\bullet(n)+1}.
}
\tag{LB}
\]

Since the reverse inequality

\[
\mathcal W_n^\star
\le
\frac{1}{2S_\bullet(n)+1}
\]

is already supplied by the Zhang–Jiang schedule, proving (LB) establishes asymptotic minimax optimality of their schedule horizon by horizon.

The quantifiers in (LB) are strict:

for every \(\varepsilon>0\), there must exist \(N_\varepsilon<\infty\) such that for every \(n\ge N_\varepsilon\) and every predetermined schedule \(h\in[0,\infty)^n\),

\[
\boxed{
\mathcal W_n(h)
\ge
\frac{1-\varepsilon}
{2S_\bullet(n)+1}.
}
\tag{Q}
\]

The threshold \(N_\varepsilon\) must be independent of the particular schedule \(h\).

The adversarial function and its dimension may depend on \(n\) and on \(h\).

---

## 4. What “matching the complexity and constant” means

A complete affirmative solution must prove (P), not merely the silver exponent.

In particular, since

\[
S_\bullet(n)=\Theta(n^\rho),
\]

(P) automatically implies the optimal polynomial convergence exponent

\[
\rho=\log_2(1+\sqrt2).
\]

It also matches the complete asymptotic constant profile, including any genuine oscillation of \(S_\bullet(n)/n^\rho\).

For reference, define

\[
\bar\nu_\bullet
:=
\liminf_{n\to\infty}
\frac{S_\bullet(n)}{n^\rho}
\]

and

\[
C_{\mathrm{ZJ}}
:=
\frac{1}{2\bar\nu_\bullet}.
\]

Then

\[
\limsup_{n\to\infty}
n^\rho U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}
=
C_{\mathrm{ZJ}}.
\]

Therefore (P) implies

\[
\boxed{
\limsup_{n\to\infty}
n^\rho\mathcal W_n^\star
=
C_{\mathrm{ZJ}}.
}
\]

However, proving only this limsup identity is weaker than (P) and does **not** count as a complete affirmative solution.

Likewise, proving merely

\[
\mathcal W_n^\star=\Theta(n^{-\rho})
\]

does not count.

---

## 5. Important warning about the number \(0.4239\)

Do not formulate the theorem as

\[
\mathcal W_n^\star
\sim
0.4239\,n^{-\rho}.
\]

That is not the precise Zhang–Jiang theorem.

Their rigorous asymptotic statement is expressed through

\[
\bar\nu_\bullet
=
\liminf_{n\to\infty}
S_\bullet(n)/n^\rho
\]

and the coefficient

\[
(2\bar\nu_\bullet)^{-1}.
\]

The frequently quoted number \(0.4239\) comes from an additional numerical estimate for \(\bar\nu_\bullet\), and is not itself the exact rigorously identified asymptotic constant.

The paper also notes numerical evidence for still smaller constants.

Thus:

- \(0.4239\) may be used as a numerical sanity check;
- it may not be assumed as an exact constant;
- a proof that only matches \(0.4239\) numerically is not sufficient;
- the rigorous benchmark is \(U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}=(2S_\bullet(n)+1)^{-1}\).

---

## 6. Current lower-bound benchmark

A recent lower bound of Ye and Liu considers, after setting \(L=1\),

\[
\mathcal R_n(h)
=
\sup_{f,x_0}
\frac{f(x_n)-f_\star}
{\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\qquad
r_n^\star
=
\inf_{h\ge0}\mathcal R_n(h),
\]

and proves that for some absolute \(C>0\),

\[
r_n^\star
\ge
n^{-\left(
\rho+
C\sqrt{\frac{\log\log n}{\log n}}
\right)}
\]

for all sufficiently large \(n\).

Equivalently, in the normalization of this problem,

\[
\mathcal W_n^\star
\ge
2
n^{-\left(
\rho+
C\sqrt{\frac{\log\log n}{\log n}}
\right)}.
\]

This establishes the correct polynomial exponent only up to a subpolynomial loss.

It does **not** establish (P).

Removing the exponent loss alone is also not sufficient: the leading asymptotic constant/profile must be matched.

---

## 7. Existing hard-function tool

The Ye–Liu lower bound may be used as a starting point.

For checkpoints

\[
1\le t_1<\cdots<t_k\le n,
\]

write

\[
b_i=h_{t_i},
\qquad
s_i
=
\sum_{j=t_{i-1}+1}^{t_i-1}h_j,
\]

with \(t_0=0\), and let \(s_{k+1}\) denote the sum of the steps after the last checkpoint.

For suitable parameters \(\epsilon,\gamma>0\), putting

\[
c=\sqrt{1+2\gamma+\epsilon^2},
\]

their construction yields constants \(a=a(\epsilon,\gamma)\) and \(B_0=B_0(\epsilon)\) such that, when every checkpoint obeys \(b_i\ge B_0\),

\[
\mathcal R_n(h)
\ge
\frac{1}{4(1+s_{k+1})}
\prod_{i=1}^k
\left[
\frac{b_i}
{c(a+s_i)+\epsilon b_i}
\right]^2.
\]

Hence

\[
\boxed{
\mathcal W_n(h)
\ge
\frac{1}{2(1+s_{k+1})}
\prod_{i=1}^k
\left[
\frac{b_i}
{c(a+s_i)+\epsilon b_i}
\right]^2.
}
\tag{Y--L}
\]

You may sharpen, replace, or completely abandon this construction.

Do not assume that merely optimizing its existing parameters is sufficient.

A central obstruction in the current proof is that the auxiliary constant \(a(\epsilon,\gamma)\) deteriorates rapidly when parameters are sent toward the regime needed to recover the exact silver exponent. Any proposed constant-level proof must track such losses quantitatively and cannot hide them inside \(o(1)\) unless that \(o(1)\) is genuinely negligible relative to \(U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}\).

---

## 8. Acceptable complete resolutions

Assume for purposes of this task that the question admits a rigorous resolution, but do not assume in advance that the answer to (P) is affirmative.

### Affirmative resolution

Prove

\[
\lim_{n\to\infty}
\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}
{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}
=
1.
\]

Because the upper bound is known, the substantive requirement is the uniform lower bound (Q).

A complete proof must apply to every sufficiently large horizon \(n\) and every predetermined nonnegative schedule of length \(n\).

### Negative resolution

Prove rigorously that (P) is false.

For example, it is sufficient to establish that there exist

\[
\delta>0,
\qquad
n_j\to\infty,
\qquad
h^{(j)}\in[0,\infty)^{n_j},
\]

such that

\[
\mathcal W_{n_j}\!\left(h^{(j)}\right)
\le
(1-\delta)
U_{n_j}^{\mathrm{ZJ}}
\]

for every \(j\), with a rigorous worst-case upper-bound certificate.

Equivalently, it is sufficient to prove

\[
\liminf_{n\to\infty}
\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}
{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}
<1.
\]

Numerical evidence is not a negative resolution.

Failure to prove the lower bound is not evidence that it is false.

---

## 9. Results that do not count as a solution

Do **not** stop for any of the following:

- reproducing the known \(n^{-\rho-o(1)}\) lower bound;
- proving \(n^{-\rho-\varepsilon}\) for every fixed \(\varepsilon>0\);
- proving only that \(\rho\) is the optimal polynomial exponent;
- proving \(\mathcal W_n^\star=\Theta(n^{-\rho})\);
- obtaining an unspecified positive constant times \(n^{-\rho}\);
- obtaining a constant strictly smaller than the Zhang–Jiang benchmark;
- proving the desired constant only along \(n=2^m-1\);
- proving it along any other subsequence;
- proving only a limsup or liminf lower bound;
- proving only
  \[
  \limsup n^\rho\mathcal W_n^\star=C_{\mathrm{ZJ}};
  \]
- showing that a candidate hard instance works for the silver schedule but not for every schedule;
- restricting stepsizes to a bounded interval;
- assuming stepsizes are monotone, ordered, separated, or bounded away from zero;
- assuming the schedule has a prescribed recursive/composable form;
- assuming all large steps can be selected as checkpoints without proving this;
- proving a lower bound only for Zhang–Jiang's class of concatenated schedules;
- proving a lower bound only for “basic”, “composable”, periodic, symmetric, or silver-like schedules;
- replacing nonnegative schedules by positive schedules without treating zero steps;
- permitting the adversary to choose the schedule after seeing the function;
- allowing adaptive stepsizes, momentum, averaging, restarting, or additional state;
- proving a result for gradient norm rather than last-iterate objective gap;
- solving the anytime version instead of the finite-horizon non-anytime problem;
- changing the performance normalization;
- dropping the factor \(1/2\) in the Zhang–Jiang normalization;
- treating \(0.4239\) as an exact proven constant;
- using floating-point calculations as proof of an asymptotic constant;
- producing a stronger lower bound than the Zhang–Jiang upper bound without noticing the resulting contradiction;
- hiding a subpolynomial loss inside a claimed constant;
- obtaining a bound of the form
  \[
  n^{-\rho}\exp[-o(\log n)]
  \]
  rather than
  \[
  (1-o(1))U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}};
  \]
- proving finite-\(n\) inequalities for finitely many computed horizons;
- providing only a conjectured extremal schedule;
- providing only a conjectured hard function;
- reducing the problem to an unproved statement of essentially the same minimax strength;
- reporting a promising lemma, numerical pattern, or “substantial progress”.

Intermediate positive progress is not a stopping condition.

---

## 10. Proof standards

Every invoked theorem must be stated with the hypotheses needed here.

In particular, carefully verify:

- convexity;
- \(L\)-smoothness;
- existence of a minimizer;
- the exact GD recursion;
- nonnegativity of all stepsizes;
- the distinction between a schedule chosen for a fixed horizon and an infinite anytime schedule;
- the normalization by \(L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2/2\);
- uniformity over every schedule \(h\in[0,\infty)^n\);
- whether constants are absolute or parameter dependent;
- every use of \(o(1)\), including its sign and uniformity;
- every limit interchange;
- every passage between \(\mathcal R_n\) and \(\mathcal W_n\);
- every use of finite-dimensional interpolation;
- every claim that a constructed collection of values and gradients is realizable by a convex \(L\)-smooth function.

A lower-bound construction may depend on \(n\) and on the entire predetermined schedule \(h\), because the worst-case function is chosen after the schedule.

Its dimension may grow with \(n\).

---

## 11. Promising proof families to investigate

Use genuinely different approaches rather than repeatedly varying one incomplete argument.

Relevant families include:

- exact smooth-convex interpolation and primal PEP constructions;
- dual PEP certificates and complementary slackness;
- deriving a dual dynamic program to the Zhang–Jiang concatenation recursion;
- minimax/Bellman formulations in which \(\varphi\) and \(\psi\) arise from primal-dual optimality;
- hard functions whose recursive composition exactly mirrors ConPP/ConPD;
- sharper checkpoint constructions without the \(\epsilon\)-dependent constant explosion;
- curved-gradient or Moreau-envelope constructions with exact rather than approximate transfer factors;
- piecewise quadratic or Huber-type interpolation constructions;
- finite-dimensional resisting-oracle constructions;
- composition/duality of extremal first-order methods;
- exact characterization of the PEP value for arbitrary nonnegative stepsizes;
- identification of a scalar functional \(V(h)\) satisfying both a universal lower bound and the same concatenation recursion as \(S_\bullet\);
- structural reductions proving that an arbitrary schedule can be decomposed at essentially no constant loss;
- induction on \(n\) with a sharp split inequality;
- continuous scaling limits of the recursion, but only when converted back to rigorous discrete estimates with the correct constant;
- exact algebraic or semialgebraic certificates for small horizons that reveal the correct general recursion.

Do not assume one of these approaches must succeed.

---

## 12. Multiagent search protocol

Use multiagent v2 aggressively and dynamically, with up to four concurrent agents if available.

Begin with a genuinely diverse portfolio.

Do not tell most early agents what the currently favored proof route is.

Maintain an explicit registry of mathematical approach families, organized by actual mechanism rather than wording.

If multiple agents converge to the same checkpoint construction, PEP dual, or recurrence argument, redirect some agents to genuinely different formulations.

Keep both possibilities alive:

1. the Zhang–Jiang schedule is asymptotically minimax optimal;
2. another schedule beats its constant/profile.

Do not discard the second possibility merely because the exponents match.

When an approach reaches a missing lemma whose strength is essentially equivalent to (Q), mark the route as blocked. Do not call that lemma “technical” or “routine”.

Only reopen such a route if a new mechanism appears.

---

## 13. Computational agents

Use computation as an adversarial and discovery tool, not as proof.

For moderate \(n\), agents should attempt to compute or tightly bound:

\[
\mathcal W_n^\star,
\qquad
U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}},
\qquad
\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}},
\]

using exact or high-precision PEP/SDP/branch-and-bound methods where possible.

They should also:

- compute \(S_\circ(n)\) and \(S_\bullet(n)\);
- inspect the maximizing split indices;
- search for schedules beating \(h_\bullet^{(n)}\);
- test proposed universal hard-function inequalities;
- search for counterexamples to intermediate lemmas;
- inspect primal and dual PEP optimizers;
- identify rank patterns and active interpolation constraints;
- attempt rational/algebraic reconstruction of numerical certificates;
- verify normalization factors independently.

A numerical counterexample to a proposed lemma is useful and should kill that lemma.

A numerical confirmation of the main conjecture is not a proof.

---

## 14. Adversarial audit

Every proposed complete affirmative proof must be independently attacked for at least the following failure modes:

- it actually proves only the exponent;
- a fixed \(\varepsilon>0\) remains in the exponent;
- \(\varepsilon=\varepsilon_n\to0\) creates a hidden constant blowup;
- it proves only infinitely many horizons;
- it proves only dyadic horizons;
- its \(o(1)\) depends on the schedule;
- its hard function is not globally convex and smooth;
- the interpolation data are inconsistent;
- the minimizer used in the normalization is not valid;
- the dimension is implicitly infinite without justification;
- very large stepsizes are excluded;
- zero stepsizes break the argument;
- a checkpoint-selection lemma silently loses a constant;
- a product inequality loses a nonvanishing factor;
- a factor \(2\) is lost when translating between normalizations;
- the Zhang–Jiang constant is replaced by \(0.4239\);
- a limsup statement is mistaken for horizon-by-horizon asymptotics;
- a numerical observation is used inside a supposedly analytic proof;
- the proof establishes optimality only within a restricted schedule family;
- a PEP dual certificate is feasible only numerically rather than exactly;
- strong duality is invoked without verifying its hypotheses;
- an asymptotic recurrence is substituted for the exact discrete one;
- the final lower bound exceeds a known rigorous upper bound because of a normalization error.

A proof survives only if all such issues are resolved explicitly.

---

## 15. Output requirements

Do not return an exploratory essay.

Do not return a research diary.

Do not return a collection of partial lemmas.

Do not return a “most promising approach”.

Do not return merely because several approaches fail.

Do not return merely because agents agree that the problem is difficult.

Do not return a positive intermediate result.

Return only one of the following:

### A. Complete affirmative proof

A rigorous proof of

\[
\lim_{n\to\infty}
\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}
{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}
=
1,
\]

including every necessary lemma and every constant/normalization check.

### B. Complete negative proof

A rigorous proof that the above limit is not \(1\), with an explicit asymptotic separation or another logically conclusive disproof.

Before returning either answer, subject the argument to an independent adversarial audit and repair every identified gap.

If the argument still contains an unproved lemma of theorem-level strength, the problem is not resolved and you must continue working.

Do not label a missing minimax inequality, uniformity statement, interpolation theorem, structural decomposition, or constant-preserving recursion as “routine”.

The stopping criterion is mathematical completion, not positive progress.

---

## 16. Literature usage

The following sources may be treated as background starting points, but their statements and normalizations must be checked carefully:

- Zehao Zhang and Rujun Jiang, *Accelerated Gradient Descent by Concatenation of Stepsize Schedules*, arXiv:2410.12395 / SIAM Journal on Optimization.
- Yuhan Ye and Kaizhao Liu, *Silver Rate Is (Almost) Optimal for Gradient Descent*, arXiv:2609.09152 and the accompanying lower-bound exposition.
- standard smooth-convex interpolation and PEP results;
- standard results concerning the silver stepsize schedule and concatenation constructions.

Public search may be used for standard named theorems, definitions, and the cited papers.

Do not use public search merely to locate a posted solution to this exact benchmark.

The task is to produce and verify the mathematical resolution itself.

## DOC-000002 — handoff_20261010/01_完整命题与假设.md

来源：D:\Danus\runtime\handoffs\gd-exact-profile-20261010\01_完整命题与假设.md

原始字节数：3343；原始 SHA-256：3bbf585653af6b6e3dc056b2bbd6db6e5eb858876c411be133af44ae5341c604

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000002 -->
# 1. 完整命题与假设

本项目研究：预先确定的非负步长梯度下降，是否在最后一次迭代的目标函数误差上，渐近精确匹配 Zhang–Jiang 构造的最佳常数曲线。

完整原始任务逐字保存在 [PROBLEM.md](PROBLEM.md)。其 SHA-256 为 `3ecc3159a696e8528ebc727776d42f657cfbc6842e9212b28c870fcd05edb89d`，与最初用户附件及导入副本一致。下文是方便新系统阅读的数学整理，不修改原任务。文献归一化的校正见第 3 份文件。

## 原始模型

固定 $L>0$。函数 $f:\mathbb R^d\to\mathbb R$ 为凸、可微，梯度 Lipschitz 常数至多为 $L$，并且存在最小点。维数 $d$ 可以是任意有限正整数。

给定迭代长度 $n$，先选择有限非负步长序列

\[
h=(h_0,\ldots,h_{n-1})\in[0,\infty)^n.
\]

它可以依赖 $n$，不能依赖随后选择的函数、初始点或算法运行中看到的梯度、函数值。允许零步长，也允许任意大的有限步长。算法只有

\[
x_{t+1}=x_t-\frac{h_t}{L}\nabla f(x_t),\qquad 0\le t<n.
\]

这里没有动量、重启、平均、附加状态或自适应步长。评价的是实际最后一点 $x_n$，不是最好的一步或迭代平均。

对每个原始选择的最小点 $x_\star\in\arg\min f$ 和不同的初始点 $x_0\ne x_\star$，定义

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)=\sup_{d,f,x_\star,x_0}
\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_\star)}{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\qquad
\mathcal W_n^\star=\inf_{h\in[0,\infty)^n}\mathcal W_n(h).
\]

上确界覆盖全部上述对象。对手可以在看完整个 $h$ 后选择函数、维数和初始点；选择步长的人不能反过来看到函数后再改步长。若使用无分子因子 2 的 $\mathcal R_n$，本项目固定 $\mathcal W_n=2\mathcal R_n$。存在多个最小点时，不能随意改变原始分母；使用最近最小点必须说明相应的最优化关系。

## 精确基准

记 $\lambda=1+\sqrt2$、$\rho=\log_2\lambda$。为避免只比较一个小数常数，保留完整递推：

\[
\Omega(x,y)=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,
\qquad
G(x,y)=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2.
\]

令 $C_0=B_0=1$，对于每个整数 $n\ge1$，

\[
C_n=\max_{0\le k<n}\Omega(C_k,C_{n-k-1}),
\qquad
B_n=\max_{0\le k<n}G(C_k,B_{n-k-1}).
\]

最大值中的 $k$ 遍历全部整数切分。它们与原文记号满足 $C_n=1+S_\circ(n)$、$B_n=1+2S_\bullet(n)$。定义

\[
U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}=\frac1{B_n}.
\]

Zhang–Jiang 的 dominant schedule 达到精确最坏值 $U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}$，从而 $\mathcal W_n^\star\le U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}$。本项目要判断

\[
\boxed{\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}=1.}
\]

这要求匹配完整常数曲线，包括可能存在的随尺度变化的振荡。不能把 $U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}$ 替换成 $0.4239n^{-\rho}$，也不能把全部整数切分自行替换为平衡切分。

## 量词必须保留

肯定方向的缺失内容等价于：对每个 $\eta>0$，存在仅依赖 $\eta$ 的 $N_\eta$，使每个 $n\ge N_\eta$ 和每个 $h\in[0,\infty)^n$ 都满足

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)\ge\frac{1-\eta}{B_n}.
\]

一个硬函数可以依赖 $n,h$，维数可以增长；阈值 $N_\eta$ 不能依赖具体的 $h$。仅在某些步数、某类递归步长或某类函数上证明结论，不能满足这个量词。

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000002 -->

## DOC-000003 — handoff_20261010/02_目标版本与成功标准.md

来源：D:\Danus\runtime\handoffs\gd-exact-profile-20261010\02_目标版本与成功标准.md

原始字节数：3307；原始 SHA-256：f07ce8c86559477fdd34fc0a60997fe71d827c18fb20ae8dac2d787c081113ae

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000003 -->
# 2. 目标版本与成功标准

截至本包整理，原始命题 OPEN。已有 18 份旧 Danus 接受事实均为有明确适用范围的支撑结论；没有完整肯定证明或完整否定证明。

## 可接受的完整结果

**肯定结果**：证明第 1 份文件中的统一下界，对所有充分大的整数步数和全部非负步长序列成立；与已有 Zhang–Jiang 上界合并得到比值趋于 1。

**否定结果**：例如构造固定 $\delta>0$、$n_j\to\infty$ 及预定非负序列 $h^{(j)}$，并对全部原始函数类证明

\[
\mathcal W_{n_j}(h^{(j)})\le(1-\delta)U_{n_j}^{\mathrm{ZJ}}.
\]

否定方向的子序列足够；肯定方向的子序列下界不够。上式需要真实的最坏情形上界证书，不能由一批跑得较快的函数样本推出。

## 目前最具体的否定接口，仍为条件式

对每个 enclosing depth $m\ge1536$，令

\[
\epsilon_m=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\epsilon_m,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\epsilon_m),\quad a=\beta/c,
\]
\[
H_0^{(m)}=(),\qquad H_{j+1}^{(m)}=(H_j^{(m)},1+ac^j,H_j^{(m)}).
\]

所有子块继承同一 $m$ 的参数；记 $S_j=\sum H_j^{(m)}$、$n_m=2^m-1$、$D_m=1+2S_m$。注意这个 $S_j$ 是候选族的质量，不是基准最优序列的 $S_\circ(j)$。

旧 Danus 接受事实 `931b9653ce0f7201` 给出

\[
C_n\le(n+1)^\rho,\qquad B_n\le\frac{12}{5}(n+1)^\rho,
\qquad \frac{B_{n_m}}{D_m}<\frac{47}{50}.
\]

因此若能对所有充分大的 $m$ 证明完整函数类上界

\[
\mathcal W_{n_m}(H_m^{(m)})\le\frac{17}{16D_m},
\]

便得到比值子序列小于 $799/800<1$，从而否定原命题。**上述全函数类上界尚未证明**。接受事实原文将全 $m\ge1536$ 写入条件；只要求充分大的 $m$ 也能通过同一逐点比较得到所需渐近结论。

## 目前最具体的上界证明输入，仍未闭合

用 $\widehat C_j=1+S_j$ 避免与基准 $C_n$ 混淆，需对每个充分大的 $m$、每个 $1\le j<m$ 及每条实际光滑凸轨迹证明

\[
E_j:=2\sum_{i<N_j}h_i(f_i-f_{N_j})
-\sum_{i<N_j}h_i(h_i-1)\|g_i\|^2
-\left(\frac7{10}\widehat C_j^2-\widehat C_j\right)\|g_{N_j}\|^2\ge0,
\quad N_j=2^j-1,
\]

此处归一化 $L=1$。已经写成完整草稿的 PD 拼接能从此输入推出总权重 $1+(32/17)S_m$ 的完整优势证书；其标量余项行列式有统一正下界。但此输入的全深度成立性没有证明，它是路线的真实缺口。

## 不能据此宣布完成

- 只得到 Silver 指数、$\Theta(n^{-\rho})$、未知正常数或只有 limsup 的结果。
- 只覆盖递归、basic、composable、正步长、有限范围步长或某个特殊函数族。
- 有限 PEP 数值、有限步数精确算例、局部最优性或未经证明的 SDP 可行性。
- 仅把原题换成另一个同等难的全称命题。
- 将“每个固定 $j$，足够大 $m$”交换成“足够大 $m$，所有 $j<m$”。
- 把一个证书模板失败说成原命题失败，或把某类硬函数太弱说成全函数类上界。

完整候选需要给出假设、实际对象、全量词、原始分母、所有常数和依赖链，并经过独立审读。旧系统接受或新系统 verifier 接受均不等同于证明助手形式化；准备对外使用的数学结果仍需人类核查。

# 3. 相关文献和材料

原始任务的文献约定、来源核对及本包文件共同构成检索入口。旧草稿中的引用和结论仍须核对假设，不能仅因被记录就当成可直接使用的定理。

| 优先级 | 文献 | 本项目中的作用 | 本地材料 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 必读 | Zehao Zhang、Rujun Jiang, *Accelerated Gradient Descent by Concatenation of Stepsize Schedules*, arXiv:2410.12395v2 | 精确基准、primitive/dominant 定义、ConPP/ConPD 拼接及等号条件 | `materials/literature/Zhang_Jiang_2410.12395v2.pdf` |
| 必读 | Yuhan Ye、Kaizhao Liu, *Silver Rate Is $Almost$ Optimal for Gradient Descent*, arXiv:2609.09152v2 | 已知接近 Silver 指数的下界、checkpoint 硬函数、参数损失 | `materials/literature/Ye_Liu_2609.09152v2.pdf` |
| 重要 | *Composing Optimized Stepsize Schedules for Gradient Descent*, arXiv:2410.16249 | f/g/s-composable 定义、递归组合与全空间证书思路 | `materials/literature/Composing_Schedules_2410.16249.pdf` |
| 重要 | Altschuler–Parrilo, Silver stepsize 工作，arXiv:2309.16530 | 原始 Silver 构造和证明机制 | `materials/literature/Silver_2309.16530.pdf`（来自已有研究资产） |
| 基础 | Smooth strongly convex interpolation / PEP，arXiv:1502.05666 | 有限梯度、函数值和位置数据的实现与性能估计 | `materials/literature/Interpolation_1502.05666.pdf`（已有副本） |
| 基准背景 | Liu–Chen–Jiang–Wang, *Optimal Recursive Composition and Dyadic Phase Laws for Gradient Descent with Predetermined Stepsizes*, arXiv:2609.11788v1 | 递归构造内部的最优性和相位行为 | 官方链接及 `materials/source_notes/SOURCE_NOTES.md` |
| 可选方法 | Zhao, arXiv:2609.11784v1 | 特定常步长族的 s-composability / 证书方法 | 官方链接及来源笔记；没有一般变步长定理 |

原始论文链接：[Zhang–Jiang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12395)、[Ye–Liu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09152)、[组合方法](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16249)、[Silver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.16530)、[插值](https://arxiv.org/abs/1502.05666)、[相位分析](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11788)、[常步长证书](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11784)。首三份 PDF 于本次交接下载；下载 URL、字节数和哈希在 `materials/literature/DOWNLOAD_RECEIPTS.json`。未重新下载的论文保留已有副本，其原路径见资产索引。

## 必须携带的来源校正

`materials/source_notes/SOURCE_NOTES.md` 保留了早期的归一化核对：Ye–Liu v2 式 $1$ 的分母是 $(L/2)\|x_0-x_\star\|^2$，其论文记号 $R_n$ 对应本项目 $W_n$，不能机械再乘 2。Lemma 2.2 的陈述有意放松了一个因子 2；若用较强 checkpoint 系数，应引用式 $12$ 后的构造计算并匹配其假设。原始任务文本没有重写，校正与原文并列保存。

递归类内的最优性不等于所有预定步长的 minimax 最优性。Zhang–Jiang 的 primitive 与另一篇论文的 s-composable 也不能不经论证互换。来源笔记中还保存了非扩张算子、H-duality、非交叉弧、图消元及 Laplacian 方法的使用边界。

## 数学资产按证据等级阅读

1. **旧系统已接受事实**：`materials/facts/` 和 `08_事实目录.md`。先读 `931b...`、`1e0b...`、`1252...`、`b98b...`，需要时再追前驱。
2. **最新完整证明草稿**：`materials/frontier/`。R13 两个完整 `PROOF_ATTEMPT.md` 与 R12 条件 PD 拼接是当前核心。
3. **历史路线综述**：`materials/historical_reports/`，以及 R5–R12 交接。它们说明先前工作怎样走到现在，运行状态以交接日期为准。
4. **未完成线索**：`materials/interrupted/`。只能作为待推导的线索。
5. **原始计算和共享记录**：完整证据压缩包中的 `project/research/` 与 `project/global_memory/`。这些资产未在交接中逐份重审或重新运行；索引不代表证明正确。

历史 Markdown 可能含旧机器的绝对路径或指向未收录的 worker 私有目录。若链接失效，先按文件名、fact ID 或 finding ID 查询 `07_资产索引.csv` 和完整共享记录；不要由链接存在推断资产已迁入。worker 私有记忆未读取、未打包。

# 4. 已有尝试与卡住的位置

截至 2026-10-10，主目标 OPEN；旧 Danus 有 18 份接受事实，没有新增目标级完成结果。以下“草稿”表示有保存的推导且已读过，不表示 verifier 已接受。

## 已接受的主要支撑资产

| 资产 | 已有内容 | 仍然不能推出什么 |
|---|---|---|
| `931b9653ce0f7201` | 精确全整数切分基准的幂包络；候选 $H_m$ 的 $47/50$ 比较；条件 $799/800$ 分离桥梁 | 未证明 $H_m$ 的全函数类上界 |
| `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` | 固定有限步长最坏值的 origin-vertex projection-simplex 上确界表示；实际 $H_3$ 的精确值与 all-body/all-d 不等式 | 未证明高层 $H_j$ 或 $H_m$；没有统一条件数 |
| `1252b8823bb5a290` | 完整有限插值、未查询终端化简与全空间证书重选 | 不允许任意删去远距离前缀约束；不等于稀疏模板完备 |
| `d8b59c7d92425121` | affine-height 多面体 Moreau envelope 的完整有限序列上确界表示 | 不提供所需渐近常数；其梯度公式不能与 projection 模型混用 |
| `b98bb807d514e80d` | 精确单切口 Huber 下界组合及其不足 | 独立正和仍只是归一化后的加权平均，没有新耦合 |
| 其他接受事实 | 正交随机梯度障碍、复多项式谱域、有限种子放大条件、局部 primitive 最优性、有限几何反例及指定螺旋族结果 | 各有明确范围；全部原文和前驱见事实目录 |

一个重要参数区别：`5b7ff4253c38b90a` 的正向硬函数针对 $\epsilon=1/m$ 的 ruler 族；当前上界候选是 $\epsilon=1/(4m)$。不能因都写作 $H_m$ 就互相套用。`e9a55f57461f253c` 是精确有限 H6 对象，也不是当前全深度结论。

## 已尝试路线的具体卡点

| 路线 | 已有进展或失败证据 | 当前缺口 |
|---|---|---|
| 任意步长的高质量硬函数 | checkpoint、交替射线、远程偏移、相关概率/有限域/组合方案，多种实际实例与范围障碍 | 仍缺对所有高总质量步长统一、常数不损失的全轨迹构造 |
| 多项式与概率矩方法 | 已接受的谱域和正交矩障碍限定了若干简单方案 | 需真实时序插值和非零时间相关；多项式值或实 PSD Gram 本身不够 |
| 反向概率/投影构造 | 精确逆操作；贪心方差策略有成本爆炸；双点 look-ahead 停在较弱 Huber 值 | 需要可重复进入面的全局耦合，并计入原始方差/距离 |
| 多面体、螺旋与多个活动面 | 有完整静态构造及受限族精确比较；单调活动面储备和独立组件均不足 | 真正重复切换的耦合轨迹仍未达到统一目标 |
| R10–R11 投影上界拼接 | 原始辅助投影可以支付部分法向项；保留完整有符号子块能量得到恒等式 | 丢弃两边真实余量后的归纳余项在合法轨迹上为负；尚无全深度保留法 |
| R12 固定权重 A/B | 同一个静态 Huber 函数在充分大的完整奇数深度使指定优势残差为负 | 只否定这两种证书权重；其实际目标误差为 $O(S^{-2})$，没有否定原上界目标 |
| Profile C 与递增权重 | 若干具体实际轨迹通过；无环流、单一目的分布与指定正区间模态锥被排除 | 一般带环系数、完整 PSD 与实际全函数类界仍缺 |
| R13 弱 history → PD | 完整条件拼接与叶子，标量行列式有统一正下界 | 弱 history 对全部继承层数成立尚未证明 |

具体完整推导按 R5、R6、…、R13 交接查找，均在 `materials/frontier/` 中。更早的反例、数组和数值日志在完整证据包；旧的数值预算和启动脚本不构成新系统的执行授权。

## R13 最值得保留的三个结果

**第一，真实轨迹排除了保留全部弱左余量的归纳。** 令子块质量 $s$、$\widehat C=1+s$、桥步超额 $k$、$r=k/\widehat C$。在静态函数 $g(x)=\operatorname{clamp}(x,-r,1)$ 上，从原始点 $\widehat C$ 出发，左块全梯度为 1，右块全梯度为 $-r$，最后点为 $-r$。对 $\theta=7/10$，

\[
E_L=(1-\theta)\widehat C^2,quad
E_R=(1-\theta)\widehat C^2r^2,quad
E_P=\widehat C^2\{1-\theta[r(2+r)]^2\}.
\]

实际 $j\ge3$ 时 $r>\sqrt2-1$，所以 $E_P<E_L$。因此系数 $\alpha\ge1$ 的规则 (E_P\ge\alpha E_L+\beta E_R+\text{非负项}) 不成立。与此同时 $E_P>0.29\widehat C^2$，所以这不是弱 history 本身的反例。

**第二，每个固定深度有严格余量。** 在 $\epsilon=0$ 的精确 Silver word 上，标准 history 等号加终端梯度为零，递归迫使所有梯度为零。弱化后的 history 因而在归一化有限 Gram 可行集上有正最小值。紧致性与连续性给出：对每个固定 $j$，存在 $\eta_j>0,M_j<\infty$，使所有 $m\ge M_j$ 都满足 $E_j\ge(\eta_j/2)\sum_i\|g_i\|^2$。缺失的是随 $j$ 增长的定量控制；不可以交换量词。

**第三，递增权重的简单证书锥仍不够。** 对 $u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i$，整个指定正区间模态锥的完整对角条件在极限强迫 $1+r^3\le1+r/6$，与 $6r^2>1$ 矛盾。一般系数矩阵未被排除。另一份真实 Huber 分析允许增长深度的内层相位，仍得到该指定残差的统一正值；它不能证明全函数类成立。

## R14 中断，不应算作已完成的一轮

当前实际状态是 `interrupted`。其原目录只有 `TASK.md`、`budget.json`，没有 `PROOF_ATTEMPT.md` 或 `HANDOFF.md`。本包单独恢复了可见会话中的进度公式和父代理提示，存为 `materials/interrupted/R14_INTERRUPTED_NOTES.md`，统一标为未完成、待独立重推。

最有用的线索是：降低左余量系数后，只补跨块行仍会在内部长步出现负对角项，因此左子块内部证书也必须变化；整树展开的端点系数有显式乘积形式。关于“坏系数只在有界低层高度出现”的界尚没有最终完成报告，也没有修补全矩阵。不能把这些会话线索写成已经验证的结论。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000006 -->
# 5. 希望尝试或排除的路线

这是给新系统的研究判断输入，不是要求固守旧系统的选题。先阅读原问题与完整证据，再决定路线。用户最新研究偏好是直接攻克原题、借鉴 Zhang–Jiang/Silver 的精确拼接；希望多搜索可行路径，少做昂贵的例行 verify。历史模型选择最终为 gpt-6-astra / ultra，consult OFF；新系统应实际确认模型可用性，不静默更换。

## 优先尝试

| 优先级 | 具体任务 | 必须产生的新内容 | 如何判断有价值 |
|---|---|---|---|
| P0 | 修改弱 history 的全深度拼接，允许左余量系数小于 1，同时改变左内部与跨子树插值系数 | 明确的系数、全部非负性、完整原始梯度空间 PSD、叶子和全部 $j<m$ 的归纳 | 真正证明第 2 份文件中的统一输入，或给出真实增长深度反例 |
| P0 | 定量分析整棵树的有符号能量与端点乘积；检验是否能统一修补低层负项 | 随 $m,j$ 有效的常数，修补重复/重叠时的总成本，终点和原始半径的完整估计 | 即使中间用有限块，必须证明可推广到任意深度；不能止于有限步模型 |
| P1 | 从任意高总质量步长出发构造真正耦合的多切口/多活动面硬函数 | 一个静态函数、所有合法查询、全原始距离、统一 all-word 规则与精确常数 | 与已接受低质量区域合并，可覆盖完整肯定量词 |
| P1 | 一般带环插值矩阵或其他完整优势证书 | 实际矩阵和完整 PSD，超出已经排除的区间正锥、单一目的分布、无环流 | 得到全函数类上界，而非另一份矩阵可行性定义 |
| 有新机制时 | 概率、相关矩、多项式、有限域、组合方法 | 必须连接到实际光滑凸插值和原始目标；解释如何越过已有正交、独立分量和时序障碍 | 新机制改变常数级可行性，而不是给同一个缺口换记号 |

## 不应原样重跑

- 仅优化单切口 Huber 组合，或独立正和：已知不足以达到全基准。
- 零时间相关/完全正交梯度对手：已有接受障碍，只有 $O(n^{-2})$ 级目标误差。
- 固定终端种子通过同一种 ConPD 延伸就自动形成固定比例渐近增益：已有 washout 结果；需要不同机制或未满足的真实 seed 假设。
- 对当前受限 primitive 证书做同样的局部扰动，忽略已接受局部最优性结论的适用范围。
- 当前方差贪心逆构造、单调活动面冻结、独立反射储备：原始成本已经成为障碍。
- 弱 history 归纳中完整保留左弱余量，再只添加非负项；真实 Huber 轨迹已排除。
- 为固定 A/B 权重重新寻找矩阵：实际完整轨迹已经否定相应残差。
- 在同一个正区间模态锥内继续调递增权重证书，或用无环/单一全局目的分布绕开同一障碍。
- 把 PSD、矩阵负方向、值梯度数据的形式构造视为已证明可由一个真实函数实现。

这些排除都有范围。Profile C、一般带环矩阵、任意活动面轨迹及原始极限命题仍开放。新系统可以改变方向，但应说明具体越过了哪个旧障碍。

# Source normalization note — 2026-09-26

This is a bibliographic reconciliation, not a newly accepted project fact.

In [Ye–Liu v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.09152v2), equation (1) divides by (L/2)||x_0-x_*||^2, so its R_n equals this project's W_n. The attachment uses a different R convention. Its W definition and target (P)/(Q) remain unchanged.

Lemma 2.2, equation (6), states prefactor 1/[4(1+s_tail)]. However, immediately after equation (12), the constructed gap is D^2/[4(1+s_tail)], the initial distance is 1, and the paper explicitly drops a factor 2 when stating the lemma. The stronger project checkpoint coefficient 1/[2(1+s_tail)] is supported by that pre-weakening construction when all its hypotheses are matched. Cite that calculation rather than the literal lemma statement.

Theorem 1.1's displayed normalization should not be mechanically doubled. Keep explicit constants separate from the existential exponent constant.

Use one-based H_t=h_{t-1} when importing the paper's checkpoints.

## Additional literature scope, checked 2026-09-26

[Liu–Chen–Jiang–Wang, arXiv:2609.11788v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11788v1), Remark 1.2, limits optimality to recursively generated classes. Sections 2.1–2.2 explicitly distinguish ambient primitive and s-composable certificate classes despite their treewise scalar correspondence. Its phase analysis is background for the benchmark, not an all-schedule minimax proof. Any use of its asymmetric phase for this exact benchmark requires matching the recurrences and normalization.

[Zhao, arXiv:2609.11784v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11784v1) proves s-composability for a particular balanced constant-step family. Its stated certificate and sum-product hypotheses must be matched before reuse; it does not assert unrestricted schedule optimality.

Neither paper has been imported as a new Danus fact. The conditional seed-amplification candidate in research/max4_primitive_seed_amplification.md includes its needed scalar argument and leaves the required seed hypothesis unmet.

## Certificate-memory methods, screened 2026-09-26

September27 duality follow-up: SOURCE_DUAL_ARC_LEAD_20260927.md records primary checks of Shu--Wang2609.03281v1 (full slack congruence plus separate signs; tridiagonal reduced-certificate sufficient case) and Yoon--Grimmer2605.02231v2 Theorem4 (noncrossing arc characterization for optimal vertex fixed-point algorithms). The latter concerns nonexpansive residual minimax, not this GD intrinsic cone. Its dual multiplier graphs are distinct from the primal coordinate-flow graphs in C35/C39. Neither source proves general primitive reversal or the original constant-profile target.

September27 supplementary screen: intake SOURCE_LEADS_20260927_0020.md records primary-source pointers and explicit applicability limits for Liu--Ye arXiv:2609.26873v1 (strong-convex repair/bridge construction) and Bok--Altschuler's proximal silver analysis (Section3.2, Laplacian Schur-complement slack). These are optional mechanism leads, not constant-profile results or new facts. Strong-convex transfer losses, composite-operator hypotheses, opposite interpolation sign and the absence of a general re-selection theorem must all be retained.

The intake HISTORICAL_CERTIFICATE_MEMORY_LEAD.md records a bounded primary-source screen of arXiv:2207.01363, arXiv:1705.03615 and arXiv:1812.02397, with exact section pointers and scope limits. These are methodology leads for retained boundary information, not imported facts or posted resolutions of this benchmark. In particular, no conjectural completeness claim or unproved reset of an inherited filter state may be used in the project's proof.

# R13 handoff: exact target open; the next join must use less than full weak-left retention

The Goal is ACTIVE and the original problem is OPEN. All new mathematics below is an UNVERIFIED manual draft. Both R13 native calls have finished, and the parent has read their complete proofs and handoffs and checked their hashes. There is no complete candidate for formal verification.

## Original target and sufficient negative route

Keep the exact original statement `lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ = 1`, or a rigorous full-class asymptotic separation, with `W = 2R`, all integer splits in `B_n = 1/U_n^ZJ`, all finite dimensions, every original selected minimizer/radius, and the actual last iterate. The original PROBLEM SHA-256 remains `3ecc3159a696e8528ebc727776d42f657cfbc6842e9212b28c870fcd05edb89d`.

For each enclosing `m >= 1536`, every child uses the SAME parameters

    epsilon = 1/(4m), beta = 1-epsilon,
    c = (1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a = beta/c,
    H_0 = (), H_(j+1) = (H_j, 1+a*c^j, H_j),
    S_j = sum H_j, C_j = 1+S_j, k_j = a*c^j.

Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies the benchmark implication: a full-class bound `W(H_m) <= 17/[16(1+2S_m)]` for all sufficiently large m gives a strict `799/800` subsequence separation. This upper premise remains unproved. Accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` supplies finite-word projection transfer and the actual H3 law, not higher-depth positivity.

## The conditional PD join survives; its weak history input is still missing

The parent note `R12_RELAXED_PD_JOIN_LEAD_20261009.md`, SHA `f7a80cbc05ee2da4b168f61fbc307817550865fa94978fcbfe067f1dae125c09`, and the independent R13 calculation agree on the exact retained PD join. If every required H_j satisfies

    E_j = 2 sum h_i(f_i-f_end) - sum h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||^2
          - [(7/10) C_j^2-C_j] ||g_end||^2 >= 0,

then a dominance induction with total `1+(32/17)S_m` closes the sufficient negative route. The scalar join determinant exceeds `851 C_j^2/196000`. This is a conditional implication, not an established all-depth input.

The new history proof derives the complete three-vector original-row remainder, then gives an actual obstruction to all rules retaining the weak left residual with coefficient at least one. For child mass s, C=1+s, bridge excess k and r=k/C, use the static asymmetric Huber function with gradient `clamp(x,-r,1)` and original start C. The whole left child has gradient 1, the bridge lands at -k, the whole right child has gradient -r, and the original final point/gradient is -r. For theta=7/10,

    E_L = (1-theta) C^2,
    E_R = (1-theta) C^2 r^2,
    E_P = C^2 [1-theta {r(2+r)}^2].

At every actual internal j>=3, r>sqrt(2)-1, so E_P<E_L. Thus `E_P >= alpha E_L + beta_R E_R + nonnegative original rows + PSD` is impossible for alpha>=1 and beta_R>=0, even with arbitrary original rows and an unrestricted full PSD remainder. Taking j=m-2 gives unbounded inherited depth. Yet E_P>(29/100)C^2: the history assertion itself is NOT refuted.

The exact standard-history recurrence is

    E_P^std = E_L^std + B E_R^std
               + ell(A_v+A_y+Q_vy+k Q_yv)
               + Delta (||q||^2-||p||^2),
    ell = (C+k)/(C-k), B = 1+2ell,
    Delta = B k^2-C^2
          = C^2(1+r)(r^2+2r-1)/(1-r).

Here A_v and A_y sum the actual bridge-to-right and terminal-to-right interpolation rows with physical h weights. This identity was derived independently by parent and native agent. The signed right energy and the defect cannot be discarded.

## What the new positive result actually says

For EACH FIXED j, there are eta_j>0 and M_j<infinity such that for all m>=M_j and all actual H_j(m) histories,

    E_j >= (eta_j/2) sum_(i=0..2^j-1) ||g_i||^2.

At exact Silver parameters epsilon=0, the standard leaf and join are nonnegative. Equality plus terminal gradient zero recursively forces every gradient to be zero. Adding the weak coefficient's `(3/10) C_j^2 ||g_end||^2` produces strict positivity on every nonzero finite interpolation dataset. Normalize its finite gradient Gram matrix to trace one; the endpoint rows bound all function differences. Compactness and continuity prove the displayed statement uniformly over ambient dimension.

No estimate on eta_j or M_j is supplied. The quantifiers cannot be exchanged. A violation at m tending to infinity would require j(m) tending to infinity. This rejects the parent's speculative fixed-prefix absorbed negative-history mechanism, but leaves growing-depth versions open.

For the entire cone-projection class `f(x)=||P_K x||^2/2`, every physical prefix T satisfies `||x_T-z_star|| <= ||x_0-z_star||` for EVERY original `z_star in K^polar`. The proof uses gradient contraction by h-1, inherited prefix products `prod(h_i-1)<=1`, and summation by parts. It also applies to compact truncations with a nonbinding cap. It does not cover active caps or arbitrary nonhomogeneous bodies, and does not assert step-by-step distance decrease.

## Fixed increasing global weights: two scoped results

Keep S=S_m, T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, Bcert=1+(32/17)S,

    u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i (i<n), u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.

The positive cone of variable-step interval matrices is now excluded at these exact columns, including arbitrary finite positive sums and m-dependent supports/weights. The full diagonal, with the Laplacian retained, yields a central necessary condition whose enclosing-m limit is `1+r^3 <= 1+r/6`, r=sqrt(2)-1. It fails because `6r^2>1`. This is a new derivation for u_i, not an import of the old h_i-only cone result. It does not exclude all general cyclic C, or even every diagonal-H C outside that cone.

For the actual symmetric Huber function, `T_Hj([-k_j,k_j])` is contained in `[-a beta,a beta]`. At J=floor(m/2), allow any start `Y_J in [max(0,k_J-d_J),k_J]`, where `d_j=k_(j+1)-(S_j+1)`, and lift via `Y_(j+1)=S_j+1+k_j-Y_j`. These are complete actual H_m trajectories with arbitrary inner phases at growing depth. Their prescribed D_u/S_m^2 has a uniform strictly positive limit lower bound. Both parities and the original radii are retained. Hence this entire phase-changing Huber attempt fails to refute the profile. Other starts, functions and dimensions remain open.

## Recovery decision, resources, and evidence

The next constructive test should allow a coefficient strictly below one on the weak left residual and retain actual cross-left/right interpolation rows, or obtain a quantitative whole-tree coercivity estimate. Repeating the unit-left weak-history join, positive interval-cone tuning, or the covered Huber inner phases cannot close the goal. No new route is declared viable solely because its excluded alternatives have grown.

## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is to prove `lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1`, uniformly over all sufficiently large horizons and every predetermined nonnegative word, or rigorously disprove it by a full-class asymptotic separation. Here `W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||^2)` over every finite dimension, convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct start; `W=2R`. The exact benchmark is `U_n^ZJ=1/B_n`, with all integer splits in the supplied Zhang--Jiang recurrence. Neither the target nor its quantifiers change.

Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` closes the exact benchmark comparison and conditional separation threshold; `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` closes finite-word projection-simplex transfer and the actual H3 law; `b98bb807d514e80d` closes the single-cut portfolio at its stated scope. None proves the target. A candidate upper architecture is a retained PD join whose full-class history input remains unproved. Its quantitative scalar margin is derived, but does not establish that input.

The latest draft supplies an actual method failure: a weak-history composition retaining at least the whole weak left residual is impossible. It does not supply proposition failure: on that same trajectory the requested parent history remains strictly positive. A separate fixed-depth coercivity proof restricts a possible violation to internal depths growing with the enclosing depth. This is not a uniform all-depth result. PARTIAL and SUBSTANTIAL do not mean almost solved; the unmatched interface is still a theorem about the full original class.

| Item | Mathematical state |
|---|---|
| Fixed goal | UNCHANGED: exact limit one or conclusive full-class asymptotic disproof |
| Verified complete proof | NO |
| Verified counterexample to the original claim | NO |
| Signed-closed supporting components | Three scoped components: benchmark; projection transfer/H3; single-cut portfolio |
| Main missing statement | A uniform original-radius lower witness, or an all-depth full-class upper certificate |
| False or obsolete methods | YES: unit weak-left retention, named positive interval cones, A/B source profiles, specified inverse greedy and monotone-face policies |
| Highest-priority unresolved task | P0: close the weak history input using a genuine whole-tree or cross-subtree certificate |

| Sub-task | Status | Closed facts | Conditional or draft material | Main missing interface |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Exact benchmark and normalization | CLOSED | `931b9653ce0f7201` | No new premise for its own statement | None within its accepted scope |
| Finite-word projection transfer | CLOSED | `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` | Higher U laws are not implied | Uniform higher-depth inequality |
| Low-mass all-word lower region | CLOSED | `b98bb807d514e80d` | Huber bound `1/(1+2 sum h)` | Complementary high-mass words |
| High-mass exact-profile lower bound | PARTIAL | No closing fact | Inverse and coupled-face mechanisms with scoped obstructions | One legal original-radius all-word construction |
| Separating upper bound | SUBSTANTIAL | Benchmark implication only | Conditional PD join and genuine leaf inputs | Weak history at every inherited depth |

Current best proof skeleton:

1. Keep `C_0=B_0=1`, `C_n=max Omega(C_k,C_l)` and `B_n=max G(C_k,B_l)` over ALL k+l=n-1, with `Omega=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2` and `G=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2`.
2. The supplied Zhang--Jiang schedule gives `W_n^*<=1/B_n`.
3. Accepted `b98bb807d514e80d` provides the matching lower bound whenever `sum h<=(B_n-1)/2`. The other words remain uncovered.
4. Independently, keep the literal perturbed hierarchy defined below, with no child parameter reset.
5. Prove its displayed weak history input for all required inherited children; this is the central missing lemma.
6. The retained PD identity and its actual leaf then produce a full-class dominance certificate of total `1+(32/17)S_m`.
7. This yields `W(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m]<=17/[16(1+2S_m)]` at every original selected radius.
8. Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` then gives a strict `799/800` subsequence comparison and resolves the original question negatively. Steps 5--7 are not established.

Central missing lemma: for every sufficiently large integer m, set `epsilon=1/(4m)`, `beta=1-epsilon`, `c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon)`, `a=beta/c`, `H_0=()`, `H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j)`, `S_j=sum H_j`, `C_j=1+S_j`. For EVERY 1<=j<m and every original convex 1-smooth actual H_j trajectory, prove

    E_j = 2 sum h_i(f_i-f_end) - sum h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||^2
          - [(7/10) C_j^2-C_j] ||g_end||^2 >= 0.

All children inherit the same enclosing m. The conditional joining calculation, fixed-depth strict positivity and Huber calibrations do not prove this assertion. The alternative affirmative route still needs a uniform high-mass lower witness at the exact B_n profile.

## 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:

- Exact all-integer benchmark comparison and conditional full-class separation threshold — `931b9653ce0f7201`.
- Fixed-word projection-simplex supremum transfer and the actual same-parameter H3 U law — `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`.
- Exact single-cut Huber portfolio and its precise insufficiency — `b98bb807d514e80d`.

Every new result in the following table is a manual draft, not a fact-graph acceptance. Full statements and derivations are in R13_FINAL_HANDOFF_20261009.md and the two complete source proofs it identifies.

| Route | Status | Concrete reason |
|---|---|---|
| Unit or larger weak-left retention plus nonnegative residuals/rows/PSD | FALSE AS STATED | Actual asymmetric Huber gives E_P<E_L at every internal j>=3; even an unrestricted PSD remainder cannot repair that inequality |
| Positive interval-mode cone for fixed increasing physical weights | FALSE AS STATED | Actual column and full diagonal conditions force `1+r^3<=1+r/6`, but `6r^2>1`, r=sqrt(2)-1 |
| Fixed-depth negative history followed by absorption as m tends to infinity | FALSE AS STATED | Strict fixed-depth Silver coercivity transfers to all sufficiently large enclosing m |
| Farther-original-minimizer absorption for cone projections | FALSE AS STATED | Every physical prefix remains within its original distance to every original polar minimizer |
| Critical Huber saturated outer lift with arbitrary permitted inner phase at floor(m/2) | OBSOLETE | Its prescribed increasing-profile residual has a uniform positive order-S_m^2 margin |
| Fixed decreasing profiles A and B | FALSE AS STATED | Prior actual complete odd-depth Huber runs have negative D_u for every admissible certificate matrix |
| Specified inverse variance greedy and nested-face reserve strategies | FALSE AS STATED | Prior full trajectory costs or exact nested-face ceilings fail the benchmark |

General nonseparable matrices, growing-depth nonhomogeneous trajectories, and the original proposition are not excluded by these statements.

## 2. Interface contract table

### Weak history to the original separating upper bound

**Input required.** The exact central missing lemma for every inherited child, together with the genuine full-class leaf dominance input. **Output claimed.** Fixed nonnegative dominance weights of total `1+(32/17)S_m`, hence the original-radius upper bound and separation. **Available facts.** `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies only the final benchmark implication. **Missing verification on the actual model.** The all-depth history inequality itself; after it is proved, a finished-object audit of the entire implication. **Failure mode if ignored.** Primitive positivity at perturbed higher levels would be assumed rather than proved. **Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### Exact Silver coercivity to perturbed growing depth

**Input required.** At epsilon zero the leaf and original-row join imply standard history positivity; equality with zero terminal gradient forces all gradients zero. **Output claimed.** For each FIXED j, some eta_j>0 and M_j<infinity give `E_j>=(eta_j/2) sum ||g_i||^2` for every m>=M_j and every finite-dimensional actual history. **Available facts.** No fact graph entry certifies this new draft. **Missing verification on the actual model.** Quantitative constants controlling j growing with m, or another uniform argument. **Failure mode if ignored.** Exchanging `for each j, eventually m` with `eventually m, for all j<m` falsely closes the goal. **Status.** PARTIAL.

### A non-unit weak-history join to all-depth preservation

**Input required.** Actual interpolation rows connecting original left and right queries, and full child inequalities with exactly their warranted coefficients. **Output claimed.** A preservation rule allowing a weak-left coefficient below one while restoring the prescribed physical function-value coefficients and proving the complete remainder nonnegative. **Available facts.** None supplies this construction; R13's actual obstruction only excludes coefficient at least one. **Missing verification on the actual model.** Actual coefficients, every row sign, the full gradient-space remainder, the leaf and a uniform induction. **Failure mode if ignored.** A missing function-value coefficient or a signed temporal-moment term can invalidate the claimed identity. **Status.** UNKNOWN.

### Full increasing-profile matrix to a dominance certificate

**Input required.** S=S_m, T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, `u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i` before the terminal query, `u_n=1+(32/17)S-sum u_i`; an actual zero-row-sum C with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and columns b_i=u_i, b_n=-sum_(i<n)u_i. With Bmat the actual trajectory matrix and L the graph Laplacian of weights `u_j(u_i-h_i)`, require FULL PSD of `R=L+diag(u_i(1-u_i)+b_i-L_ii)-(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T)`. **Output claimed.** Nonnegative original dominance residual and the same separating upper bound. **Available facts.** `931b9653ce0f7201` only after the upper premise. **Missing verification on the actual model.** Actual C outside the excluded families and the full uniform PSD proof. **Failure mode if ignored.** A positive Laplacian is only one summand; the whole positive interval cone already fails the full diagonal. **Status.** PARTIAL.

### Legal high-mass witness to an affirmative exact-profile bound

**Input required.** One static admissible function and complete actual trajectory for every high-mass nonnegative word, including zero and arbitrarily large finite steps, at the original selected radius. **Output claimed.** `W_n(h)>=(1-o(1))/B_n` uniformly in h. **Available facts.** `b98bb807d514e80d` closes only the complementary low-mass region. **Missing verification on the actual model.** A globally coupled constant-preserving construction and an all-word selection rule. **Failure mode if ignored.** Changing a wall can change a previously recorded gradient; using another minimizer can silently replace the original radius. **Status.** PARTIAL.

## 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

“The weak history holds at every fixed depth, so it holds at j=m.” **DANGEROUS.** The compactness constants depend on j without a bound. Growing depth is the missing content, not an endpoint convention.

“Retain the weak left residual in full and repair the bridge by more positive rows.” **FALSE AS STATED.** The actual Huber construction has E_P<E_L. This excludes even arbitrary positive original rows and a full PSD remainder under that retention coefficient.

“The fixed increasing weights have a positive Laplacian, so diagonal compensation can be tuned inside the interval cone.” **FALSE AS STATED.** The new obstruction uses the FULL diagonal after the Laplacian cancellation. General matrices remain outside its scope.

“A positive Huber phase test proves the profile.” **DANGEROUS.** The complete covered Huber family has a positive margin, but other starts, functions and dimensions are not included.

“A cone distance bound proves the weak history input.” **DANGEROUS.** The cone argument rules out a specific absorbed negative-history mechanism; it does not establish the terminal objective and gradient terms for arbitrary functions.

- Do not repeat the unit-left weak-history join with different bridge/right coefficients: the actual obstruction covers all such nonnegative representations.
- Do not retune a positive interval-mode combination for the fixed increasing profile: the entire named cone fails.
- Do not use a bounded internal-depth scan or continuity limit as a uniform induction.
- Do not call a free negative matrix direction an actual smooth-convex trajectory.
- Do not restart any child with new enclosing parameters or replace its original minimizer/radius.
- Do not promote the conditional PD identity, exact benchmark implication, or a scoped draft exclusion to the original answer.

## 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 — close the weak history at growing inherited depth

**Target statement.** The exact central missing lemma in Section 0, for all sufficiently large m and every 1<=j<m. **Prerequisites.** Original nonnegative interpolation rows, true child coefficients and same enclosing parameters. **Existing facts to use.** `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies the consequence only; no accepted fact supplies the new input. **Missing checks.** A non-unit left-retention construction with actual cross-subtree rows, or a quantitative whole-tree bound on the retained signed defects, with explicit constants sufficient for the three-tenths terminal loss. **Closure criterion.** A complete dimension-uniform original-space inequality covering all required j, followed by the retained PD induction. This is the primary upper-route bridge; it is independent of the affirmative lower construction.

### Bridge 2 — instantiate a full global dominance certificate

**Target statement.** For all sufficiently large m, construct the exact increasing-profile C and prove the complete R from Section 2 PSD, or supply another fully specified original-radius dominance certificate of the same sufficient total. **Prerequisites.** The actual physical trajectory matrix, all column/row constraints and nonnegative interpolation coefficients. **Existing facts to use.** `931b9653ce0f7201` for the final comparison. **Missing checks.** Genuine coefficients outside the excluded positive interval and single-destination families; full positivity rather than separate convenient signs. **Closure criterion.** The full original-class upper bound, not its matrix reformulation. This is an independent sufficient upper route.

### Bridge 3 — attain the exact lower profile on every high-mass word

**Target statement.** For every eta>0, all sufficiently large n and every nonnegative word with `sum h>(B_n-1)/2`, construct an original admissible instance with payoff at least `(1-eta)/B_n`, with threshold independent of h. **Prerequisites.** One final fixed function, every physical query and the original radius; correlations must survive all face updates. **Existing facts to use.** `b98bb807d514e80d` for the complementary words and `1252b8823bb5a290` for full interpolation scope. **Missing checks.** A globally coupled witness cost and a constant-preserving uniform selection rule. **Closure criterion.** Every quantifier of the original lower bound follows. This is independent of the two upper routes and remains theorem-strength.

## DOC-000011 — project/fact_graph/facts/931b9653ce0f7201.md

来源：D:\Danus\runtime\projects\gd-exact-profile-20260926\fact_graph\facts\931b9653ce0f7201.md

原始字节数：13858；原始 SHA-256：ca6979471107970b95f495239043768f76a276bceae6d723559ad746a12b978d

证据标签：historical_fact_file_check_original_scope。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000011 -->
---
fact_id: 931b9653ce0f7201
problem_id: gd-exact-profile-20260926
author: max4
predecessors: [b98bb807d514e80d]
glossary_introduces:
  A_be,j: Hierarchy mass 1+S_j for the fixed enclosing integer m.
  D_m: Hierarchy mass 1+2S_m for the stated same-enclosing-m word.
  E_be: Positive scalar auxiliary E_be(t)=(1+t)^rho-1 for t>0.
  N_be: Positive real split total u+w; in the induction u=k+1 and w=n-k.
  Psi_be: Scalar function (5/6)x^(19/15)+(1-x)^(19/30) on [0,1].
  Q_be: Natural logarithm in logarithmic coordinates, Q_be(z)=log(E_be(exp z)) for real z.
  T_be: Primitive comparison trial (x^(1/rho)+y^(1/rho))^rho for positive x,y.
  W_n_star: Infimum of W_n(h) over all nonnegative words h of length n; the original minimax value.
  g_be: Critical-value comparison function (1-x/2)/(1-x)^(11/30) on [0,2/3].
  kappa_be: Constant 12/5 in the dominant capacity envelope.
  n_m: Actual physical horizon 2^m-1 of H_m.
  r_be: The larger nonnegative root (sqrt(y)+sqrt(y+8x))/2 for x,y>=0.
  v_be: Hierarchy coefficient a/(c-2)=beta/[c(c-2)], strictly between 0 and 1.
external_refs: []
---

## statement
A tighter exact all-split benchmark envelope and a conditional GD certificate threshold.

Fix L>0. For every integer n>=0 and predetermined nonnegative finite word h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)), run gradient descent
x_(t+1)=x_t-(h_t/L)*grad f(x_t), 0<=t<n.
Let W_n(h) be the supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2) over every finite Euclidean dimension, every convex differentiable function f with L-Lipschitz gradient and an attained minimizer x_star, and every x_0!=x_star. Let R_n(h) be the same supremum without the numerator factor 2, so W_n(h)=2R_n(h). Put W_n_star=inf_(h in [0,infinity)^n) W_n(h), including the empty word at n=0.

Define lambda=1+sqrt(2), rho=log_2(lambda). For nonnegative real x,y put
Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,
G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2.
Define C_0=B_0=1 and, for EVERY integer n>=1,
C_n=max_(integer 0<=k<n) Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1)),
B_n=max_(integer 0<=k<n) G(C_k,B_(n-k-1)).
These are the original primitive and dominant benchmark capacities, with EVERY integer split retained. Put U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

For every integer n>=0,
C_n<=(n+1)^rho and B_n<=(12/5)(n+1)^rho.

For each integer m>=1536, set epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=lambda(1+epsilon), and a=beta/c. Define the finite words
H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), 0<=j<m.
The same enclosing m, epsilon, beta, c, and a are used at every child. Here the hierarchy symbols H_j,a,c are local to this statement. Put S_j=sum H_j, n_m=2^m-1, and D_m=1+2S_m. Then H_m is a predetermined strictly positive word of length n_m and
B_(n_m)/D_m<47/50
for every integer m>=1536.

Finally, the following is a CONDITIONAL implication, with its entire hypothesis left unproved: IF
W_(n_m)(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) for EVERY integer m>=1536,
as a bound on the full function-class supremum W defined here, THEN for every such m,
B_(n_m) W_(n_m)(H_m)<799/800,
W_(n_m)_star/U_(n_m)^ZJ<799/800,
and
liminf_(n->infinity) W_n_star/U_n^ZJ<=799/800<1.

No unconditional full-function upper bound on W_(n_m)(H_m) is asserted. The unconditional conclusions are the two all-n benchmark envelopes and the hierarchy benchmark ratio. The original unrestricted minimax problem remains unresolved by this result alone.

## proof
The accepted predecessor b98bb807d514e80d is used only to identify the original benchmark coordinates and normalization: C_n=S_circle(n)+1, B_n=2S_bullet(n)+1, the displayed Omega and G recurrences, and the factor-2 definition of W_n. All inequalities needed here are proved in this proof. No saved numerical enclosure, evaluated recurrence, other accepted estimate, or unverified finding is used.

1. Exact exponent and positivity.

The identity lambda^2=2lambda+1 gives lambda(lambda-2)=1 and lambda^3=7+5sqrt(2). Since 2>49/25, sqrt(2)>7/5, so lambda^3>14. Therefore
lambda^15>14^5=537824>524288=2^19.
Taking the increasing base-2 logarithm gives rho>19/15>1. In particular rho/2>19/30. Also sqrt(2)<3/2, whence lambda<5/2 and lambda^2=2lambda+1<6.
The finite recurrences define positive finite capacities: C_0=B_0=1, and Omega and G map positive finite pairs to positive finite numbers. Each maximum is over a nonempty finite set when n>=1.

2. Primitive power inequality, including zero arguments.

For t>0 let E_be(t)=(1+t)^rho-1. It is positive. For z in the real line set Q_be(z)=log E_be(exp z), where log is natural. With t=exp z,
Q_be'(z)=rho*t*(1+t)^(rho-1)/E_be(t).
Since dt/dz=t, differentiation and collection of factors gives
Q_be''(z)
=rho*t*(1+t)^(rho-2)*[(1+rho*t)E_be(t)-rho*t*(1+t)^rho]/E_be(t)^2
=rho*t*(1+t)^(rho-2)*[(1+t)^rho-1-rho*t]/E_be(t)^2.
The function of t in the last square bracket is zero at t=0 and has derivative rho*((1+t)^(rho-1)-1)>0 for t>0. Consequently Q_be''(z)>0 for every real z. Convexity applied at z and -z yields
Q_be(z)+Q_be(-z)>=2Q_be(0).
Exponentiating and using 2^rho=lambda gives
E_be(t)*E_be(1/t)>=E_be(1)^2=(lambda-1)^2=2 for every t>0.

Take x,y>0, let u=x^(1/rho), w=y^(1/rho), and T_be=(u+w)^rho. Then
T_be-x=x E_be(w/u), T_be-y=y E_be(u/w).
Multiplying proves (T_be-x)(T_be-y)>=2xy, or
T_be^2-(x+y)T_be-xy>=0.
The roots of the latter polynomial are
[(x+y) +/- sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy)]/2.
For x,y>0 the smaller root is negative, the larger is Omega(x,y), and T_be>0. Thus T_be>=Omega(x,y). If one of x,y is zero, Omega(x,0)=x and Omega(0,y)=y, so the same inequality is equality; at (0,0) both sides are zero. This proves
Omega(x,y)<=(x^(1/rho)+y^(1/rho))^rho for all x,y>=0.

The polynomial x^2+6xy+y^2 is nondecreasing in each nonnegative variable, as is x+y. Monotonicity of the square root shows that Omega is nondecreasing in each argument on the whole nonnegative quadrant. For the primitive induction, C_0=1=(0+1)^rho. Suppose the bound is proved at all indices below n>=1. For every integer k with 0<=k<n, both k and n-k-1 are below n, so
Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1))
<=Omega((k+1)^rho,(n-k)^rho)
<=(k+1+n-k)^rho=(n+1)^rho.
Taking the maximum over every allowed integer k proves C_n<=(n+1)^rho.

3. A scalar inequality that will control every dominant split.

For every real p>=19/30 and x in [0,1], we claim
(5/6)x^(2p)+(1-x)^p<=1.
The exponents are positive, so zero bases present no ambiguity. Both summands are nonincreasing functions of p for each fixed x in [0,1]. It suffices to establish the claim at p=19/30. Define the continuous function
Psi_be(x)=(5/6)x^(19/15)+(1-x)^(19/30), 0<=x<=1.
It has endpoint values Psi_be(0)=1 and Psi_be(1)=5/6. On the open interval,
Psi_be'(x)=(19/18)x^(4/15)-(19/30)(1-x)^(-11/30).
Thus each interior critical point satisfies
x^(4/15)*(1-x)^(11/30)=3/5,
equivalently x^8*(1-x)^11=(3/5)^30.
For x in (0,1), logarithmic differentiation of x^8*(1-x)^11 gives
8/x-11/(1-x)=(8-19x)/(x(1-x)).
It is strictly negative for x>8/19. Since 2/3>8/19, every x in [2/3,1) satisfies
x^8*(1-x)^11<=2^8/3^19<(3/5)^30.
For the strict inequality, multiply positive denominators and use the exact integer comparison
2^8*5^30=238418579101562500000000
<239299329230617529590083=3^49.
It follows that every interior critical point of Psi_be is in (0,2/3).

At an interior critical point, the derivative equation gives
(5/6)x^(19/15)=(x/2)*(1-x)^(-11/30).
Hence its value is
Psi_be(x)=(1-x/2)*(1-x)^(-11/30)=g_be(x).
The right side defines a positive continuously differentiable function on [0,2/3]. Its logarithmic derivative is
g_be'(x)/g_be(x)=-1/(2-x)+11/[30(1-x)]
=(19x-8)/[30(1-x)(2-x)].
The denominator is positive on [0,2/3]. Therefore g_be decreases on [0,8/19] and increases on [8/19,2/3]. Its maximum on [0,2/3] is at an endpoint. At the endpoints,
g_be(0)=1,
g_be(2/3)^30=2^30/3^19<1,
where 2^30=1073741824<1162261467=3^19.
Because g_be(2/3)>0, this proves g_be(2/3)<1. In particular every interior critical value of Psi_be is at most 1.
The continuous function Psi_be attains a maximum on [0,1]. If a maximizing point is an endpoint the value is at most 1 by the endpoint calculation; if it is interior its derivative vanishes and the critical-value argument applies. This proves Psi_be<=1 and therefore the claimed scalar inequality for all p>=19/30 and x in [0,1]. No differentiability at x=1 is required.

4. Dominant power inequality and induction over all splits.

Put kappa_be=12/5. For x,y>=0 define
r_be=(sqrt(y)+sqrt(y+8x))/2.
Direct squaring yields
r_be^2=y/2+2x+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2=G(x,y).
Thus sqrt(G(x,y))=r_be, the larger nonnegative root of
z^2-sqrt(y)z-2x=0.
The other root is nonpositive. For a positive trial z, if this polynomial is nonnegative, then z is at least its larger root. This includes x=0 or y=0; when both vanish the larger root is zero.
The formula for G is coordinatewise nondecreasing on x,y>=0, because its linear terms and its square-root radicand have that property.

We prove B_n<=kappa_be(n+1)^rho by strong induction. At n=0 this is 1<=12/5. Suppose it holds below n>=1. For each original integer split k with 0<=k<n set u=k+1, w=n-k, and N_be=u+w=n+1. Then u,w>=1 and the primitive bound already proved gives
G(C_k,B_(n-k-1))<=G(u^rho,kappa_be*w^rho).
Take the positive trial z=sqrt(kappa_be)*N_be^(rho/2). The root polynomial for this last G value is nonnegative at z if and only if
kappa_be*N_be^rho-kappa_be*w^(rho/2)*N_be^(rho/2)-2u^rho>=0,
or, after division by the positive quantity kappa_be*N_be^rho,
(2/kappa_be)*(u/N_be)^rho+(w/N_be)^(rho/2)<=1.
Apply part 3 with x=u/N_be in (0,1) and p=rho/2>19/30. Because 2/kappa_be=5/6 and w/N_be=1-x, that part gives exactly this inequality. Consequently
G(u^rho,kappa_be*w^rho)<=z^2=kappa_be(n+1)^rho.
The same bound holds for EVERY split k, so its maximum B_n satisfies it. This completes the dominant induction, including n=0.

5. Exact mass of the same-enclosing-m hierarchy.

Fix an arbitrary integer m>=1536. The parameters epsilon,beta,c,a are those in the statement and do not change with j. They satisfy
0<epsilon=1/(4m)<=1/6144<1/8,
0<beta<1, c>lambda>2, a>0.
Every bridge 1+a*c^j is strictly positive. Length starts at zero and satisfies length(H_(j+1))=2length(H_j)+1, so induction gives length(H_j)=2^j-1 for all 0<=j<=m.
Summing the literal concatenation gives S_0=0 and
S_(j+1)=2S_j+1+a*c^j.
Define A_be,j=1+S_j for 0<=j<=m and v_be=a/(c-2)=beta/[c(c-2)].
Then A_be,0=1 and A_be,j+1=2A_be,j+a*c^j.
For all these indices the exact solution is
A_be,j=(1-v_be)2^j+v_be*c^j.
Indeed the initial value is 1, and substituting the right side into the recurrence gives
(1-v_be)2^(j+1)+(2v_be+a)c^j
=(1-v_be)2^(j+1)+v_be*c^(j+1),
using v_be(c-2)=a.

For t>2, t(t-2) is strictly increasing since its derivative is 2t-2>0. Thus c>lambda>2 and lambda(lambda-2)=1 imply c(c-2)>1. Since 0<beta<1, one has 0<v_be<1, so A_be,m>=v_be*c^m.

Expanding c=lambda(1+epsilon) gives
c(c-2)=1+2(lambda+1)epsilon+lambda^2 epsilon^2
<1+7epsilon+6epsilon^2,
where the strict upper bound uses lambda<5/2 and lambda^2<6.
The numerator beta=1-epsilon is positive, so
v_be>(1-epsilon)/(1+7epsilon+6epsilon^2).
Furthermore
(1-8epsilon)(1+7epsilon+6epsilon^2)
=1-epsilon-50epsilon^2-48epsilon^3<1-epsilon.
Division by the positive denominator shows v_be>1-8epsilon=1-2/m>0.

The binomial expansion at integer m>=2 has nonnegative terms. Its first three terms give
(1+1/(4m))^m
>=1+1/4+[m(m-1)/2]/(16m^2)
=41/32-1/(32m)>0.
Also 2^m>m for integers m>=1: at m=1 this is 2>1; if 2^m>m then 2^(m+1)>2m>=m+1. Since lambda>2, lambda^m>m and lambda^(-m)<1/m.

Now D_m=1+2S_m=2A_be,m-1 is positive. Using the exact A_be,m formula, then the positive lower bounds just proved, yields
D_m/lambda^m
>=2v_be*(1+1/(4m))^m-lambda^(-m)
>2(1-2/m)*(41/32-1/(32m))-1/m
=41/16-99/(16m)+1/(8m^2)
>41/16-99/(16m)
>=41/16-99/(16*1536)
=20959/8192
>120/47.
The last strict comparison follows from
20959*47=985073>983040=8192*120.
Every division and multiplication in this chain is by a positive quantity, and m=1536 is included.

At the actual horizon n_m=2^m-1, part 4 gives
B_(n_m)<=(12/5)(2^m)^rho=(12/5)lambda^m.
Combining the upper bound on B with the strict lower bound on D gives
B_(n_m)/D_m<(12/5)/(120/47)=47/50.
The strictness is retained because D_m/lambda^m>120/47 and B_(n_m)>0. This proves the unconditional hierarchy comparison with the SAME enclosing parameters at every child.

6. Conditional conclusion and normalization.

The definitions of W_n and R_n have identical suprema and differ only by multiplication of each nonnegative payoff by 2. Multiplication by this positive constant commutes with the supremum, so W_n=2R_n. We use W throughout, with no rescaling of the supplied hypothetical bound.

For this part ONLY, assume the full-class upper bound
W_(n_m)(H_m)<=17/(16D_m)
for every integer m>=1536. H_m is an admissible predetermined word of length n_m, so the infimum defining W_(n_m)_star satisfies
W_(n_m)_star<=W_(n_m)(H_m).
Because B_(n_m)>0 and U_(n_m)^ZJ=1/B_(n_m), part 5 gives
W_(n_m)_star/U_(n_m)^ZJ
=B_(n_m)*W_(n_m)_star
<=B_(n_m)*W_(n_m)(H_m)
<=(17/16)*B_(n_m)/D_m
<(17/16)*(47/50)=799/800.
The same displayed chain also proves B_(n_m)*W_(n_m)(H_m)<799/800.

For every integer cutoff N there is an m>=1536 with n_m>=N, since n_m=2^m-1 tends to infinity. Thus the infimum of W_n_star/U_n^ZJ over all n>=N is at most 799/800. Taking the increasing limit of these tail infima proves
liminf_(n->infinity) W_n_star/U_n^ZJ<=799/800<1.
This conclusion is conditional on the full-class upper hypothesis, which has not been established in this proof. Only the benchmark envelopes and the hierarchy benchmark comparison are unconditional.

## intuition
A logarithmic-coordinate convexity identity bounds the primitive join at the silver exponent. An exact critical-point argument gives the 12/5 dominant envelope. The inflated same-m hierarchy has enough scalar mass for a 47/50 benchmark ratio; converting this into a minimax improvement still requires the explicitly unproved full-function upper hypothesis.
